<div class="content" id="age-requirements">
<h1>Child and youth passengers</h1>
<section class="generic">
<p>The definition of "Child" and "Youth" varies by country and operator. This is why we ask for the age of young passengers.</p>
<p>Sometimes children below a certain age can travel without a seat for free. If you want to guarantee a seat for child passengers, enter '6' as the age of the child.</p>
<p><a class="tracked" href="https://help.loco2.com/article/299-article" target="_blank">Read more about child and youth passenger ages.</a> See also <a class="tracked" href="https://help.loco2.com/article/616-article" target="_blank">youth discounts and railcards</a>.</p>
</section>
